<li><a href="#quarkus-messaging-pulsar-now-depends-on-quarkus-avro"><code>quarkus-messaging-pulsar</code> now depends on <code>quarkus-avro</code></a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<a href="#for-extension-and-quarkus-developers">For extension and Quarkus developers</a>
<ul>
<li><a href="#core">Core</a></li>
<li><a href="#independent-projects">Independent Projects</a></li>
<li><a href="#devtools">Devtools</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<table role="table">
<tbody><tr>
<td>
<div>Note</div>
</td>
<td>
<div>
<p>We highly recommend the use of <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/update-quarkus" rel="nofollow"><code>quarkus update</code></a> to update to a new version of Quarkus.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Items marked below with ⚙️ ✅ are automatically handled by <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/update-quarkus" rel="nofollow"><code>quarkus update</code></a>.</p>
</div>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-general" class="heading-element">General</h2><a id="user-content-general" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: General" href="#general"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-java-21-as-the-minimum-version" class="heading-element">Java 21 as the minimum version</h3><a id="user-content-java-21-as-the-minimum-version" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Java 21 as the minimum version" href="#java-21-as-the-minimum-version"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Starting with Quarkus 4, Java 21 is the minimum version to use.
Java 25 is recommended.
Java 17 is not supported anymore.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-ubi-10-for-container-images" class="heading-element">UBI 10 for container images</h3><a id="user-content-ubi-10-for-container-images" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: UBI 10 for container images" href="#ubi-10-for-container-images"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>By default, the container images are now based on UBI 10.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-config-gear-white_check_mark" class="heading-element">Config ⚙️ ✅</h3><a id="user-content-config-gear-white_check_mark" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Config :gear: :white_check_mark:" href="#config-gear-white_check_mark"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>The <code>quarkus.vertx.use-async-dns</code> configuration property has become a build-time configuration property. That means that, moving forward, a rebuild is required for any changes to take effect.</p>

<div>
<p>In the <code>quarkus.log.handler</code> rows, <code>&lt;type&gt;</code> is <code>console</code>, <code>file</code>, <code>syslog</code> or <code>socket</code>. The <code>init-task-defaults</code> and <code>init-tasks</code> rows also apply to the <code>quarkus.openshift</code> and <code>quarkus.knative</code> prefixes.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you are still using any of these deprecated configurations, update to their replacements. Remember to check any <code>ConfigSource</code> in use (<code>application.properties</code>, System, Environment, <code>pom.xml</code>, or others in use). These removed properties will be flagged by Quarkus as being unused.</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<p>The compatibility layer that still accepted the <code>quarkus.package.*</code> properties renamed in Quarkus 3.9 has been removed:</p>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.package.type</code> is no longer recognized. For a JAR type, set <code>quarkus.package.jar.type</code> to the same value. For <code>native</code>, set <code>quarkus.native.enabled=true</code>; for <code>native-sources</code>, also set <code>quarkus.native.sources-only=true</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.package.compress-jar</code>, <code>filter-optional-dependencies</code>, <code>add-runner-suffix</code>, <code>user-configured-ignored-entries</code>, <code>user-providers-directory</code>, <code>included-optional-dependencies</code>, <code>include-dependency-list</code>, <code>decompiler.enabled</code>, <code>decompiler.jar-directory</code>, <code>manifest.attributes.<strong></strong></code>, <code>manifest.sections.</code> and <code>manifest.add-implementation-entries</code> moved under <code>quarkus.package.jar</code>, for example <code>quarkus.package.jar.compress</code> and <code>quarkus.package.jar.manifest.attributes.*</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.package.decompiler.version</code> has no replacement.</p>
<div>
<p><code>quarkus update</code> has migrated these properties since Quarkus 3.10.</p>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-escaped-commas-in-configuration-values-that-use-property-expressions" class="heading-element">Escaped commas in configuration values that use property expressions</h4><a id="user-content-escaped-commas-in-configuration-values-that-use-property-expressions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Escaped commas in configuration values that use property expressions" href="#escaped-commas-in-configuration-values-that-use-property-expressions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Quarkus 4 upgrades to SmallRye Config 4. When a configuration value contains a <code>${…​}</code> expression, expression expansion now interprets backslashes throughout the value, not just next to the expression. Values without property expressions are unaffected by this change.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If the value is also parsed as a comma-separated list, escape each literal comma for both steps: expression expansion and list splitting. In <code>application.properties</code>, this means using four backslashes instead of two before a literal comma.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Quarkus 3
quarkus.native.additional-build-args=-H:BuildOutputJSONFile=${build.output},--initialize-at-run-time=com.example.First\\,com.example.Second

# Quarkus 4
quarkus.native.additional-build-args=-H:BuildOutputJSONFile=${build.output},--initialize-at-run-time=com.example.First\\\\,com.example.Second"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Quarkus 3</span>
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.native.additional-build-args</span>=-H:<span class="pl-k">BuildOutputJSONFile</span>=${build.output},--<span class="pl-k">initialize-at-run-time</span>=com.example.First\\,com.example.Second

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Quarkus 4</span>
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.native.additional-build-args</span>=-H:<span class="pl-k">BuildOutputJSONFile</span>=${build.output},--<span class="pl-k">initialize-at-run-time</span>=com.example.First\\\\,com.example.Second</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>Without this change, <code>com.example.Second</code> is passed to <code>native-image</code> as a separate argument and may be rejected as an unrecognized option. Specifically for <code>--initialize-at-run-time</code>, another option is to repeat the flag once per class. That works on both Quarkus 3 and 4 without escaped commas.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>See <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/config-reference#escaping" rel="nofollow">Configuration Reference: Escaping</a> and <a href="https://github.com/smallrye/smallrye-config/pull/1568">SmallRye Config #1568</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-logging" class="heading-element">Logging</h3><a id="user-content-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Logging" href="#logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-named-log-handlers-now-use-their-custom-format-when-json-logging-is-enabled" class="heading-element">Named log handlers now use their custom <code>format</code> when JSON logging is enabled</h4><a id="user-content-named-log-handlers-now-use-their-custom-format-when-json-logging-is-enabled" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Named log handlers now use their custom format when JSON logging is enabled" href="#named-log-handlers-now-use-their-custom-format-when-json-logging-is-enabled"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Previously, when the <code>quarkus-logging-json</code> extension was present, named log handlers (<code>quarkus.log.handler.console.&lt;name&gt;</code>, <code>quarkus.log.handler.file.&lt;name&gt;</code>, <code>quarkus.log.handler.syslog.&lt;name&gt;</code> and <code>quarkus.log.handler.socket.&lt;name&gt;</code>) that did not set <code>json.enabled</code> always used
the global JSON formatter.
Any <code>format</code> configured on these handlers was silently ignored.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Starting with Quarkus 4.0, a named handler that configures a custom <code>format</code> uses that format instead of inheriting the JSON formatter.
Named handlers that do not set a <code>format</code> keep inheriting the JSON configuration of their handler type, as before.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you configured a <code>format</code> on a named handler but relied on it producing JSON, your logs will now use the plain text pattern instead.
For example, with the following configuration, the access log file now contains <code>%d{yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}</code>-formatted lines instead of JSON:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.enabled=true
quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.path=target/appAccess.log
quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.format=%d{yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}"><pre><span class="pl-k">quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.enabled</span>=true
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.path</span>=target/appAccess.log
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.format</span>=%d{yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>To keep the JSON output, either remove the <code>format</code> property from the named handler, or enable JSON explicitly on it:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.json.enabled=true"><pre><span class="pl-k">quarkus.log.handler.file.access-log.json.enabled</span>=true</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>See <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/pull/58162">#58162</a> and the <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/logging#json-named-handlers" rel="nofollow">logging guide</a> for more details.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-ecs-format" class="heading-element">ECS format</h4><a id="user-content-ecs-format" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ECS format" href="#ecs-format"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>When using structured access logs with the JSON Logging core extension, the logs are now properly formatted in ECS format when the log format is defined to ECS.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>See <a href="https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/pull/57139">https://github.com/quarkusio/quarkus/pull/57139</a> for more details.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-arc" class="heading-element">ArC</h3><a id="user-content-arc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ArC" href="#arc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>ArC, the CDI container in Quarkus, now supports CDI 5.0. The following annotations are now available: <code>@jakarta.enterprise.inject.Reserve</code>, <code>@jakarta.enterprise.context.Eager</code>, and <code>@jakarta.enterprise.context.AutoClose</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The <code>@jakarta.enterprise.inject.Reserve</code> annotation is a 100% replacement of ArC’s <code>@io.quarkus.arc.DefaultBean</code>. The following elements are now deprecated for removal:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>the <code>@DefaultBean</code> annotation itself</p>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>replace with <code>@Reserve</code> and unless you have a <code>@jakarta.annotation.Priority</code> already, add <code>@jakarta.annotation.Priority(0)</code></p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<p>the <code>InjectableBean.isDefaultBean()</code> method</p>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>replace with <code>Bean.isReserve()</code></p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>The <code>@Eager</code> annotation is a simplified replacement of ArC’s <code>@Startup</code>. Note that <code>@Startup</code> is not deprecated, because it supports many more cases than <code>@Eager</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-native-image-sources-compilation-native-image-args" class="heading-element">Native Image Sources Compilation (native-image.args)</h3><a id="user-content-native-image-sources-compilation-native-imageargs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Native Image Sources Compilation (native-image.args)" href="#native-image-sources-compilation-native-imageargs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>To properly preserve spaces and special control characters in native image arguments, Quarkus now quotes and escapes the arguments written to the <code>native-image.args</code> file (which is generated when using <code>quarkus.native.sources-only</code> is set to <code>true</code>).</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Because of this formatting change, you can no longer use standard shell expansion (<code>$(cat native-image.args)</code>) to pass these arguments to the <code>native-image</code> executable. You must use GraalVM’s argument file syntax (<code>@filename</code>) instead.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you have custom scripts, CI/CD pipelines, or Docker commands that manually trigger the native compilation from the generated sources, you need to update them.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Before:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$native-image $(cat native-image.args)"><pre><span class="pl-smi">$native</span>-image <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>cat native-image.args<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span></pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>(For container builds)</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run ... -c &quot;native-image $(cat native-image.args) -J-Xmx4g&quot;"><pre>docker run ... -c <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>native-image <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">$(</span>cat native-image.args<span class="pl-pds">)</span></span> -J-Xmx4g<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>After:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ native-image @native-image.args"><pre>$ native-image @native-image.args</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>(For container builds)</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run ... -c &quot;native-image @native-image.args -J-Xmx4g&quot;"><pre>docker run ... -c <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>native-image @native-image.args -J-Xmx4g<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-http-and-rest" class="heading-element">HTTP and REST</h2><a id="user-content-http-and-rest" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: HTTP and REST" href="#http-and-rest"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-semicolon-is-no-longer-a-query-parameter-delimiter" class="heading-element">Semicolon is no longer a query parameter delimiter</h3><a id="user-content-semicolon-is-no-longer-a-query-parameter-delimiter" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Semicolon is no longer a query parameter delimiter" href="#semicolon-is-no-longer-a-query-parameter-delimiter"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The HTTP server no longer treats the semicolon (<code>;</code>) as a query parameter delimiter by default.
Only the ampersand (<code>&amp;</code>) separates query parameters. For example, the query string <code>a=1;b=2</code> now yields a single parameter <code>a</code> with the value <code>1;b=2</code>, rather than two parameters <code>a</code> and <code>b</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>To restore the previous behavior, set <code>quarkus.http.use-semicolon-as-query-param-delimiter=true</code>.
For the management interface, set <code>quarkus.management.use-semicolon-as-query-param-delimiter=true</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-jakarta-rest" class="heading-element">Jakarta REST</h3><a id="user-content-jakarta-rest" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Jakarta REST" href="#jakarta-rest"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Upgraded to Jakarta REST 4 APIs</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Partial support for Jakarta REST 4 in Quarkus REST</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Upgraded to RESTEasy 7</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>SseEventSink.close()</code> now throws <code>IOException</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>It is no longer allowed to return Quarkus REST Client <code>Response</code> or <code>RestResponse</code> responses as return values from Quarkus REST endpoints (server). This is a security precaution because most client HTTP responses need some filtering before exposing them as server responses.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-grpc" class="heading-element">gRPC</h3><a id="user-content-grpc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: gRPC" href="#grpc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The separate gRPC server has been removed. gRPC services now run on the main HTTP server (Vert.x). The dedicated gRPC port (default 9000) no longer exists and <code>quarkus.grpc.server.use-separate-server</code> has been removed.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>gRPC clients that relied on the default port 9000 must target the HTTP server port instead:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.grpc.clients.hello.port=${quarkus.http.port}"><pre><span class="pl-k">quarkus.grpc.clients.hello.port</span>=${quarkus.http.port}</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>For TLS, use <code>${quarkus.http.ssl-port}</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Server TLS is now configured through <code>quarkus.http.ssl.<strong></strong></code> instead of <code>quarkus.grpc.server.ssl.</code>. Certificate files must be filesystem paths, not classpath resources. They are no longer embedded in native executables, which allows runtime rotation without rebuilding.</p>
</div>
<table role="table">
<colgroup>
<col>
<col>
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Removed property</th>
<th>Replacement</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><p><code>quarkus.grpc.server.port</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.http.port</code></p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>quarkus.grpc.server.use-separate-server</code></p></td>
<td><p>Removed, always embedded</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>quarkus.grpc.server.ssl.certificate</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.http.ssl.certificate.files</code></p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>quarkus.grpc.server.ssl.key</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.http.ssl.certificate.key-files</code></p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<div>
<p>The other properties of the separate gRPC server were removed as well: <code>quarkus.grpc.server.host</code>, <code>test-port</code>, <code>plain-text</code>, <code>alpn</code>, <code>instances</code>, <code>handshake-timeout</code>, <code>max-inbound-metadata-size</code>, the remaining <code>ssl.<strong></strong></code> properties, and the <code>transport-security.</code>, <code>in-process.<strong></strong></code>, <code>netty.</code> and <code>xds.*</code> groups.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-serialization" class="heading-element">Serialization</h2><a id="user-content-serialization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Serialization" href="#serialization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-jackson" class="heading-element">Jackson</h3><a id="user-content-jackson" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Jackson" href="#jackson"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Quarkus 4.0 upgrades from Jackson 2 to Jackson 3.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-package-rename" class="heading-element">Package rename</h4><a id="user-content-package-rename" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Package rename" href="#package-rename"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Jackson 3 moved the core and databind packages:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><code>com.fasterxml.jackson.core</code> → <code>tools.jackson.core</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>com.fasterxml.jackson.databind</code> → <code>tools.jackson.databind</code></p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p><strong>Annotations are NOT renamed</strong> — they remain under <code>com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>User code that imports from <code>com.fasterxml.jackson.databind</code> (e.g. <code>ObjectMapper</code>, <code>JsonNode</code>, <code>JsonSerializer</code>, <code>JsonDeserializer</code>) must change to <code>tools.jackson.databind</code>.
Similarly, <code>com.fasterxml.jackson.core</code> imports (e.g. <code>JsonParser</code>, <code>JsonGenerator</code>) become <code>tools.jackson.core</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-cdi-bean-type-change" class="heading-element">CDI bean type change</h4><a id="user-content-cdi-bean-type-change" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CDI bean type change" href="#cdi-bean-type-change"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Quarkus now produces a <code>JsonMapper</code> bean instead of <code>ObjectMapper</code>.
Injecting <code>ObjectMapper</code> still works because <code>JsonMapper extends ObjectMapper</code>, but programmatic CDI lookup by the exact <code>ObjectMapper</code> type may need adjustment.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-customizer-api-rename" class="heading-element">Customizer API rename</h4><a id="user-content-customizer-api-rename" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Customizer API rename" href="#customizer-api-rename"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p><code>ObjectMapperCustomizer</code> has been renamed to <code>JsonMapperBuilderCustomizer</code>.
The API also changed: instead of receiving a mutable <code>ObjectMapper</code>, you now receive a <code>JsonMapper.Builder</code>:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Jackson 2
@Singleton
public class MyCustomizer implements ObjectMapperCustomizer {
    public void customize(ObjectMapper mapper) {
        mapper.enable(SerializationFeature.INDENT_OUTPUT);
    }
}

// Jackson 3
@Singleton
public class MyCustomizer implements JsonMapperBuilderCustomizer {
    public void customize(JsonMapper.Builder builder) {
        builder.enable(SerializationFeature.INDENT_OUTPUT);
    }
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Jackson 2</span>
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Singleton</span>
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">MyCustomizer</span> <span class="pl-k">implements</span> <span class="pl-smi">ObjectMapperCustomizer</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">void</span> <span class="pl-en">customize</span>(<span class="pl-smi">ObjectMapper</span> <span class="pl-s1">mapper</span>) {
        <span class="pl-s1">mapper</span>.<span class="pl-en">enable</span>(<span class="pl-smi">SerializationFeature</span>.<span class="pl-c1">INDENT_OUTPUT</span>);
    }
}

<span class="pl-c">// Jackson 3</span>
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Singleton</span>
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">MyCustomizer</span> <span class="pl-k">implements</span> <span class="pl-smi">JsonMapperBuilderCustomizer</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">void</span> <span class="pl-en">customize</span>(<span class="pl-smi">JsonMapper</span>.<span class="pl-smi">Builder</span> <span class="pl-s1">builder</span>) {
        <span class="pl-s1">builder</span>.<span class="pl-en">enable</span>(<span class="pl-smi">SerializationFeature</span>.<span class="pl-c1">INDENT_OUTPUT</span>);
    }
}</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>A new <code>JsonFactoryBuilderCustomizer</code> interface is also available for customizing the underlying <code>JsonFactory</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-default-behavior-changes" class="heading-element">Default behavior changes</h4><a id="user-content-default-behavior-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Default behavior changes" href="#default-behavior-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Several Jackson features changed their defaults between Jackson 2 and Jackson 3.
Quarkus provides configuration properties to control these, so you can revert to Jackson 2 behavior where needed:</p>
</div>
<table role="table">
<colgroup>
<col>
<col>
<col>
<col>
<col>
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Behavior</th>
<th>Jackson 2</th>
<th>Jackson 3</th>
<th>Quarkus property</th>
<th>Quarkus default</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><p>Fail on trailing tokens after deserialized value</p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.jackson.fail-on-trailing-tokens</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code> (Jackson 3)</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p>Fail on null for primitive types</p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.jackson.fail-on-null-for-primitives</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code> (Jackson 3)</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p>Include un-annotated properties when <code>@JsonView</code> is active</p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.jackson.default-view-inclusion</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code> (Jackson 3)</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p>Use getters of Collection/Map types as setters</p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.jackson.use-getters-as-setters</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code> (Jackson 3)</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p>Fail on empty beans (no accessors)</p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.jackson.fail-on-empty-beans</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code> (Jackson 2)</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p>Write durations as numeric timestamps</p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>false</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>quarkus.jackson.write-durations-as-timestamps</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>true</code> (Jackson 2)</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<div>
<table role="table">
<tbody><tr>
<td>
<div>Note</div>
</td>
<td>
For <code>fail-on-empty-beans</code> and <code>write-durations-as-timestamps</code>, Quarkus defaults to the <strong>Jackson 2</strong> behavior to avoid breaking existing applications.
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div>
<div>
<p>For example, to revert to Jackson 2’s lenient trailing-token behavior:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.jackson.fail-on-trailing-tokens=false"><pre><span class="pl-k">quarkus.jackson.fail-on-trailing-tokens</span>=false</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-full-list-of-changes" class="heading-element">Full list of changes</h4><a id="user-content-full-list-of-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Full list of changes" href="#full-list-of-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Keep in mind that Jackson 3 has made a lot of breaking changes over Jackson 2, and users are advised to read the full migration guide <a href="https://github.com/FasterXML/jackson/blob/main/jackson3/MIGRATING_TO_JACKSON_3.md#changes-mapperfeature">here</a></p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-persistence" class="heading-element">Persistence</h2><a id="user-content-persistence" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Persistence" href="#persistence"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-h2databasetestresource-deprecated" class="heading-element">
<code>H2DatabaseTestResource</code> deprecated</h3><a id="user-content-h2databasetestresource-deprecated" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: H2DatabaseTestResource deprecated" href="#h2databasetestresource-deprecated"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p><code>H2DatabaseTestResource</code> is now deprecated. Dev Services for H2 automatically starts an in-process database when <code>quarkus.datasource.db-kind=h2</code> is set and no JDBC URL is configured. In the case of H2, this does not require docker/podman.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>See <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/databases-dev-services" rel="nofollow">Dev Services for databases</a> for details.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-quarkus-data-hibernate" class="heading-element">Quarkus Data Hibernate</h3><a id="user-content-quarkus-data-hibernate" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quarkus Data Hibernate" href="#quarkus-data-hibernate"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-package-and-accessor-renames" class="heading-element">Package and accessor renames</h4><a id="user-content-package-and-accessor-renames" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Package and accessor renames" href="#package-and-accessor-renames"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Some packages and generated classes have been renamed to better align with the <code>record</code> wording and remove <code>*Blocking</code> suffixes.</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Replace imports from affected packages: <code>io.quarkus.data.hibernate.stateless</code> is now <code>io.quarkus.data.hibernate.record</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Replace references to default repository accessors (e.g. <code>MyEntity_.managedBlocking()</code>) and repository switchers (e.g. <code>myRepo.managedReactive()</code>): <code>managedBlocking()</code> ⇒ <code>managed()</code>, <code>statelessBlocking()</code> ⇒ <code>record()</code>, <code>statelessReactive()</code> ⇒ <code>recordReactive()</code>. <code>managedReactive()</code> is unchanged.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Replace references to generated default repository types (if any; this should be rare) to use their new name: recompile sources with the annotation processor enabled, then fix compilation errors by updating direct references to these generated types.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-hibernate-orm" class="heading-element">Hibernate ORM</h3><a id="user-content-hibernate-orm" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hibernate ORM" href="#hibernate-orm"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-hibernate-orm-default-db-version" class="heading-element">Application may fail to start due to higher default version for dialects</h4><a id="user-content-application-may-fail-to-start-due-to-higher-default-version-for-dialects" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Application may fail to start due to higher default version for dialects" href="#application-may-fail-to-start-due-to-higher-default-version-for-dialects"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Quarkus now defaults to assuming more recent versions of databases at build time, which could lead to startup errors such as the one bellow when using Hibernate ORM to connect to an older version of a database:</p>
</div>
<div>

<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="io.quarkus.runtime.configuration.ConfigurationException: Persistence unit '&lt;default&gt;' was configured to run with a database version of at least '16', but the actual version is '15.0.1'. Consider upgrading your database. The minimum version supported by the PostgreSQLDialect dialect is 15.0.0. Alternatively, rebuild your application with 'quarkus.datasource.db-version=15.0.1' (but this may disable some features and/or impact performance negatively). As a last resort, if you are certain your application will work correctly even though the database version is incorrect, disable the check with 'quarkus.hibernate-orm.database.version-check.enabled=false'
	at io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.runtime.service.QuarkusRuntimeInitDialectFactory.checkActualDbVersion(QuarkusRuntimeInitDialectFactory.java:112)
	at io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.runtime.observers.QuarkusSessionFactoryObserverForDbVersionCheck.sessionFactoryCreated(QuarkusSessionFactoryObserverForDbVersionCheck.java:15)
	at org.hibernate.internal.SessionFactoryObserverChain.sessionFactoryCreated(SessionFactoryObserverChain.java:33)
	at org.hibernate.internal.SessionFactoryImpl.&lt;init&gt;(SessionFactoryImpl.java:323)
        [...]"><pre lang="" class="notranslate"><code>io.quarkus.runtime.configuration.ConfigurationException: Persistence unit '&lt;default&gt;' was configured to run with a database version of at least '16', but the actual version is '15.0.1'. Consider upgrading your database. The minimum version supported by the PostgreSQLDialect dialect is 15.0.0. Alternatively, rebuild your application with 'quarkus.datasource.db-version=15.0.1' (but this may disable some features and/or impact performance negatively). As a last resort, if you are certain your application will work correctly even though the database version is incorrect, disable the check with 'quarkus.hibernate-orm.database.version-check.enabled=false'
	at io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.runtime.service.QuarkusRuntimeInitDialectFactory.checkActualDbVersion(QuarkusRuntimeInitDialectFactory.java:112)
	at io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.runtime.observers.QuarkusSessionFactoryObserverForDbVersionCheck.sessionFactoryCreated(QuarkusSessionFactoryObserverForDbVersionCheck.java:15)
	at org.hibernate.internal.SessionFactoryObserverChain.sessionFactoryCreated(SessionFactoryObserverChain.java:33)
	at org.hibernate.internal.SessionFactoryImpl.&lt;init&gt;(SessionFactoryImpl.java:323)
        [...]</code></pre></div>

</div>
<div>
<p>To fix this, either upgrade your database, or configure Quarkus to target an older database version — follow the instructions in the error message, or see <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/hibernate-orm/#hibernate-dialect-supported-databases" rel="nofollow">the dedicated section in the documentation</a>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The new default (minimum) database versions are (see also the documentation link above):</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>IBM Db2 12.1</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>MariaDB 11.4</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Microsoft SQL Server 16 (2022)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>MySQL 8.4</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Oracle Database 23</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>PostgreSQL 16</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you override the database version, check the <a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/dialect/dialect.html" rel="nofollow">Hibernate ORM dialect minimums</a>. CR2 raises the minimum from 14 to 15 for PostgreSQL, Postgres Plus and Spanner PostgreSQL, and from 2.6.1 to 2.7.4 for HSQLDB.</p>
</div>
<div>
<table role="table">
<tbody><tr>
<td>
<div>Note</div>
</td>
<td>
<div><strong>Why?</strong></div>
<div>
<p>When building a Quarkus application, a Hibernate dialect is determined at build time, along with the version of the database it targets, which allows generating Hibernate metadata early and reduces startup times, especially in native mode.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Historically, the version of the database has always defaulted to the earliest supported version, which maximizes compatibility with older versions of databases, but with newer versions of databases leads to sub-par performance and potentially makes some features unavailable. This is true regardless of the actual DB version being used at runtime, because that part of Hibernate’s behavior is set at build time.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The best known example of this is the many warnings issued when managing the schema on an Oracle database, because Quarkus defaulted to Oracle 19, which does not support <code>IF EXISTS</code> statements in DDL — while newer versions do.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>This change allows Quarkus to deliver the best performance and functionality by default, while preserving the ability to opt into a compatibility mode by setting the datasource’s <code>db-version</code>.</p>
</div>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-quarkus-hibernate-orm-sql-load-script-is-deprecated" class="heading-element">
<code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.sql-load-script</code> is deprecated</h4><a id="user-content-quarkushibernate-ormsql-load-script-is-deprecated" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: quarkus.hibernate-orm.sql-load-script is deprecated" href="#quarkushibernate-ormsql-load-script-is-deprecated"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The configuration property <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.sql-load-script</code> is replaced by two properties that separate initializing the schema from loading data:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.schema-management.init-script</code> (<code>schema.sql</code> by default, if present in the classpath) initializes the schema generated by Hibernate ORM and is only executed when Hibernate ORM creates the schema.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.data-management.init-script</code> (<code>data.sql</code> by default, if present in the classpath) loads data, and is executed regardless of how the schema is managed (including with Flyway or Liquibase) whenever <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.data-management.strategy</code> is <code>create</code> — by default in dev and test
modes only.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<table role="table">
<tbody><tr>
<td>
<div>Note</div>
</td>
<td>
The special value <code>no-file</code> can still be used for either property, to prevent the default <code>schema.sql</code>/<code>data.sql</code> from being picked up.
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div>
<div>
<p>The property <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.sql-load-script</code> is deprecated.
It keeps its historical behavior, and still defaults to <code>import.sql</code> in dev and test mode, if that file exists.
It cannot be combined with the new properties: setting both new and deprecated property, or having both <code>import.sql</code> and <code>schema.sql</code> or <code>data.sql</code> in the classpath, is considered ambiguous and will fail the build.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>For more details, see <a href="https://quarkus.io/version/main/guides/hibernate-orm/#sql-init-scripts" rel="nofollow">https://quarkus.io/version/main/guides/hibernate-orm/#sql-init-scripts</a>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>To migrate:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Rename <code>import.sql</code> to <code>schema.sql</code> and/or <code>data.sql</code> as relevant: <code>schema.sql</code> for schema initialization (constraints, views, functions, …​), and <code>data.sql</code> for data loading. If one of the resulting files is empty, remove it.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Do <strong>not</strong> keep a file named <code>import.sql</code> alongside <code>schema.sql</code> or <code>data.sql</code> in dev/test mode — <code>import.sql</code> enables the deprecated behavior and causes an ambiguity error.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Split any explicit use of <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.sql-load-script</code>/<code>quarkus.hibernate-orm."persistence-unit-name".sql-load-script</code> into:</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.schema-management.init-script</code>/<code>quarkus.hibernate-orm."persistence-unit-name".schema-management.init-script</code>, set to the path to your schema initialization script (if any)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.data-management.init-script</code>/<code>quarkus.hibernate-orm."persistence-unit-name".data-management.init-script</code>, set to the path to your data loading script (if any)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>If you need to keep <code>import.sql</code> in the classpath but want to use the new properties, set <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.sql-load-script=no-file</code> to disable the deprecated behavior triggered by that file.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Conversely, if you want to keep the deprecated behavior from <code>import.sql</code> but also have a <code>data.sql</code> or <code>schema.sql</code> present, either rename or delete the conflicting file, or set <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.data-management.init-script=no-file</code> and/or <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.schema-management.init-script=no-file</code> to
take those out of the picture.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Address changes in behavior in edge cases:</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>If your application relies on Hibernate ORM creating the database schema in <strong>production</strong>, and you need data loading scripts (<code>data.sql</code> or custom) to be executed in production as well, make sure to explicitly set <code>%prod.quarkus.hibernate-orm.data-management.strategy=create</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>If your application relies on Hibernate ORM creating the database schema in <strong>production</strong>, and you have a file named exactly <code>schema.sql</code> in your classpath that you do <strong>not</strong> want executed, set <code>%prod.quarkus.hibernate-orm.schema-management.init-script=no-file</code> explicitly.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>If your application relies on something else than Hibernate ORM (e.g. Flyway) creating the database schema in <strong>test</strong>/<strong>dev</strong> mode, and you have a file named exactly <code>data.sql</code> in your classpath:</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>If you <strong>do</strong> want that file executed, make sure it is idempotent (e.g. by having it delete all data before inserting data).</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>If you do <strong>not</strong> want that file executed, set <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.data-management.strategy=none</code> explicitly.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-session-metrics-logging" class="heading-element">Session metrics logging</h4><a id="user-content-session-metrics-logging" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Session metrics logging" href="#session-metrics-logging"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.log-session-metrics</code> property is deprecated and has no effect. Remove it from your configuration.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Hibernate ORM controls per-session metrics logging with the <code>org.hibernate.session.metrics</code> log category instead.
To enable it, add the following to <code>application.properties</code>:</p>
</div>
<div>

<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.log.category.&quot;org.hibernate.session.metrics&quot;.level=DEBUG
quarkus.log.category.&quot;org.hibernate.session.metrics&quot;.min-level=DEBUG"><pre>quarkus.log.category.<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>org.hibernate.session.metrics<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>.<span class="pl-k">level</span>=DEBUG
quarkus.log.category.<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>org.hibernate.session.metrics<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>.<span class="pl-k">min-level</span>=DEBUG</pre></div>

</div>
<div>
<p>Per-session metrics are not logged at the default log level. This log category is independent of <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.statistics</code>, which controls statistics collection.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The deprecated property was already ignored in Quarkus 3.40 / Hibernate ORM 7.4.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-upgrade-to-hibernate-orm-8-0" class="heading-element">Upgrade to Hibernate ORM 8.0</h4><a id="user-content-upgrade-to-hibernate-orm-80" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Upgrade to Hibernate ORM 8.0" href="#upgrade-to-hibernate-orm-80"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The Quarkus extension for Hibernate ORM was upgraded from Hibernate ORM 7.4 to 8.0, which uses Jakarta Persistence 4.0.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The following changes are most likely to affect existing applications. Refer to the <a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/" rel="nofollow">Hibernate ORM 8.0 migration guide</a> for more information.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-api-changes" class="heading-element">API changes</h5><a id="user-content-api-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: API changes" href="#api-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#session-replicate" rel="nofollow"><code>Session.replicate()</code> and <code>ReplicationMode</code> were removed</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#stateless" rel="nofollow"><code>StatelessSession.insert()</code> no longer returns an identifier, and <code>fetch()</code> now returns the fetched value</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#query-api" rel="nofollow">Query APIs distinguish selections from mutations; named mutations use <code>@NamedStatement</code> or <code>@NamedNativeStatement</code></a>. ⚙️ ✅</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#api-changes" rel="nofollow">The deprecated <code>org.hibernate.jpa.QueryHints</code> class was removed; use <code>HibernateHints</code>, <code>SpecHints</code>, or <code>AvailableHints</code> as appropriate</a>. ⚙️ ✅</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#generic-generator" rel="nofollow"><code>@GenericGenerator</code> declarations using <code>name</code> or <code>strategy</code> must be updated</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#transform-removal" rel="nofollow">The deprecated <code>org.hibernate.transform</code> package was removed</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#timeout" rel="nofollow">Query and transaction timeout APIs now use <code>jakarta.persistence.Timeout</code></a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/javadocs/org/hibernate/Session.html#lock(java.lang.Object,org.hibernate.LockMode,jakarta.persistence.LockOption%E2%80%A6%E2%80%8B)" rel="nofollow"><code>Session.lock(Object, LockOptions)</code> was removed; use <code>Session.lock(Object, LockMode, LockOption…​)</code></a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/javadocs/org/hibernate/Session.html#refresh(java.lang.Object,jakarta.persistence.RefreshOption%E2%80%A6%E2%80%8B)" rel="nofollow"><code>Session.refresh(Object, LockOptions)</code> was removed; use <code>Session.refresh(Object, RefreshOption…​)</code></a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-behavioral-changes" class="heading-element">Behavioral changes</h5><a id="user-content-behavioral-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Behavioral changes" href="#behavioral-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#xml-validation" rel="nofollow">Correct schema violations in <code>orm.xml</code> or <code>hbm.xml</code> that Hibernate 8.0 CR1 accepted. Quarkus now reports them at build time</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#column-on-composite-attribute" rel="nofollow">If bootstrap rejects <code>@Column</code> on a non-aggregate embedded attribute, use <code>@AttributeOverride</code> on the embedding property to name the affected columns</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#query-type-mismatch" rel="nofollow">Query result types are checked more strictly</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#session-get" rel="nofollow"><code>EntityHandler.get()</code> throws <code>EntityNotFoundException</code> for missing entities instead of returning <code>null</code> as the former <code>Session.get()</code> did</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#filtered-to-one-associations" rel="nofollow">To-one associations are nulled out when <code>@SQLRestriction</code> or filters with <code>applyToLoadByKey = true</code> hide the target</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#graph-flushing-behavior" rel="nofollow">Graph-based flushing can change the order or shape of SQL statements</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#validation-lifecycle" rel="nofollow">Jakarta Validation lifecycle events have changed; insert-time validation groups use <code>pre-insert</code> instead of <code>pre-persist</code></a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#xml-dtd-external-entities" rel="nofollow">XML parsing disables DTD processing and external entities by default</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#follow-on-skip-locked" rel="nofollow">Skip-locked requests are rejected when follow-on entity locking is required</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#follow-on-locking-missing-rows" rel="nofollow">Missing rows during non-skipping follow-on locking cause an optimistic-lock failure</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#pessimistic-refresh-visibility" rel="nofollow">Refreshing a pessimistically locked entity may retain a locking read</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#sqlserver-geography-axis-order" rel="nofollow">SQL Server geography values use the corrected axis order; previously written values may need migration</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#enhancement-synthetic-fields" rel="nofollow">Enhancer-generated fields are now synthetic</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>The <code>Session.get()</code> comparison only applies when upgrading directly from Quarkus 3.23 or older: <a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/7.0/migration-guide/#defer-to-jpa" rel="nofollow"><code>Session.get()</code> was removed in Quarkus 3.24 / Hibernate ORM 7.0</a>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If graph-based flushing causes problems, the legacy strategy remains temporarily available through <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.unsupported-properties."hibernate.flush.queue.type"=legacy</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-ddl-changes" class="heading-element">DDL changes</h5><a id="user-content-ddl-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: DDL changes" href="#ddl-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#set-null-uniqueness" rel="nofollow">Unique constraints for sets with nullable elements may change</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#validation-column-length" rel="nofollow">Column length uses the smallest explicit maximum among applicable validation constraints</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#audited-inverse-collection-index" rel="nofollow">With Hibernate core auditing, a <code>mappedBy</code> one-to-many whose index Hibernate writes needs its order or map-key columns in the element audit table. Add those columns and backfill the latest audit rows before resuming writes</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/orm/8.0/migration-guide/#sqlserver-time-precision" rel="nofollow">On SQL Server, an explicit fractional-second precision now generates <code>time(n)</code>. Check existing values before narrowing a column previously generated as <code>time</code></a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-hibernate-reactive" class="heading-element">Hibernate Reactive</h3><a id="user-content-hibernate-reactive" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hibernate Reactive" href="#hibernate-reactive"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-hibernate-reactive-default-db-version" class="heading-element">Application may fail to start due to higher default version for dialects</h4><a id="user-content-application-may-fail-to-start-due-to-higher-default-version-for-dialects-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Application may fail to start due to higher default version for dialects" href="#application-may-fail-to-start-due-to-higher-default-version-for-dialects-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>See <a href="#hibernate-orm-default-db-version">Hibernate ORM database version guidance</a> for details.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-upgrade-to-hibernate-reactive-5-1" class="heading-element">Upgrade to Hibernate Reactive 5.1</h4><a id="user-content-upgrade-to-hibernate-reactive-51" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Upgrade to Hibernate Reactive 5.1" href="#upgrade-to-hibernate-reactive-51"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The Quarkus extension for Hibernate Reactive was upgraded from Hibernate Reactive 4.5 to 5.1.
Hibernate Reactive 5.1 uses Hibernate ORM 8.0, so the ORM migration notes above also apply to reactive persistence units and Hibernate Reactive with Panache.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The following reactive-specific changes are most likely to affect existing applications. Refer to the <a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/" rel="nofollow">Hibernate Reactive 5.1 migration guide</a> for more information.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-api-changes-1" class="heading-element">API changes</h5><a id="user-content-api-changes-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: API changes" href="#api-changes-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#flush-mode" rel="nofollow">Query-level flush mode methods now accept <code>jakarta.persistence.QueryFlushMode</code>; session-level <code>setFlushMode(FlushMode)</code> is unchanged</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#create-query-typed" rel="nofollow"><code>Session.createQuery(TypedQueryReference)</code> now returns <code>SelectionQuery</code> instead of <code>Query</code></a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div>
