<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-behavioral-changes-1" class="heading-element">Behavioral changes</h5><a id="user-content-behavioral-changes-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Behavioral changes" href="#behavioral-changes-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#find-get-query-producer" rel="nofollow">Reactive <code>get()</code> throws <code>EntityNotFoundException</code> for missing entities; use <code>find()</code> to obtain <code>null</code></a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#jdbc-time-zone-times" rel="nofollow">If <code>quarkus.hibernate-orm.jdbc.timezone</code> differs from the JVM default, convert SQL <code>TIME</code> values written by earlier Reactive versions from the old JVM zone to the configured zone. Reactive CR3 uses that setting when reading and writing times</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#current-transaction" rel="nofollow"><code>Mutiny.Session.currentTransaction()</code> and <code>Stage.Session.currentTransaction()</code> detect externally started transactions</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#rollback-only" rel="nofollow">Failed operations mark transactions for rollback even if the failure is recovered, subject to Jakarta Persistence exception exemptions</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#extended-lock-scope" rel="nofollow"><code>PessimisticLockScope.EXTENDED</code> also locks owned collection and join-table rows</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#unique-key-proxies" rel="nofollow">Associations referencing unique keys fetch uninitialized proxies before writing the foreign key</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/reactive/5.1/migration-guide/#query-cache" rel="nofollow">Cacheable queries now read cached results instead of always querying the database</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-hibernate-search" class="heading-element">Hibernate Search</h3><a id="user-content-hibernate-search" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hibernate Search" href="#hibernate-search"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-upgrade-to-hibernate-search-9-0" class="heading-element">Upgrade to Hibernate Search 9.0</h4><a id="user-content-upgrade-to-hibernate-search-90" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Upgrade to Hibernate Search 9.0" href="#upgrade-to-hibernate-search-90"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The Quarkus extensions for Hibernate Search were upgraded from 8.4 to 9.0.
Existing indexes can still be read and written without reindexing, and outbox-polling tables do not need a schema update.
Refer to the <a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/search/9.0/migration/html_single/" rel="nofollow">Hibernate Search 9.0 migration guide</a> for more information.</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/search/9.0/migration/html_single/#api" rel="nofollow">The deprecated <code>Search.toJpaQuery()</code> and <code>Search.toOrmQuery()</code> adapters were removed</a>. Use the Hibernate Search query DSL to execute search queries and retrieve results instead.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/search/9.0/migration/html_single/#api" rel="nofollow">The deprecated automatic-indexing synchronization API was removed</a>. Replace <code>SearchSession.automaticIndexingSynchronizationStrategy(…​)</code> with <code>SearchSession.indexingPlanSynchronizationStrategy(…​)</code>, and implementations of <code>AutomaticIndexingSynchronizationStrategy</code> with <code>IndexingPlanSynchronizationStrategy</code>. For configuration, use <code>quarkus.hibernate-search-orm.indexing.plan.synchronization.strategy</code> (or <code>quarkus.hibernate-search-standalone.indexing.plan.synchronization.strategy</code> for the standalone mapper).</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><a href="https://docs.hibernate.org/search/9.0/migration/html_single/#behavior" rel="nofollow">Exceptions from property getters and constructors are no longer wrapped in the same <code>SearchException</code></a>. Review exception handling and tests that depend on the old wrapper or cause chain.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-redis-client" class="heading-element">Redis client</h3><a id="user-content-redis-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Redis client" href="#redis-client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The interface <code>RedisHostsProvider</code> used to have a method <code>Set&lt;URI&gt; getHosts()</code>. This method now returns <code>Collection&lt;URI&gt;</code>. This change is binary incompatible, but it is source compatible. You don’t have to change your <code>RedisHostsProvider</code> implementations.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-flyway" class="heading-element">Flyway</h3><a id="user-content-flyway" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Flyway" href="#flyway"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Flyway has been upgraded from 12.0.0 to 13.10.0.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>This upgrade moves Flyway from Jackson 2 (<code>com.fasterxml.jackson</code>) to Jackson 3 (<code>tools.jackson</code>).</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The <code>flyway-database-mongodb</code> artifact has been renamed to <code>flyway-database-nc-mongodb</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-observability" class="heading-element">Observability</h2><a id="user-content-observability" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Observability" href="#observability"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-micrometer" class="heading-element">Micrometer</h3><a id="user-content-micrometer" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Micrometer" href="#micrometer"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Make sure we register http.server.requests always with the same tags. Micrometer will now send a warning if we register the same metric more than once with different Tag names.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-use-prometheus-client-v1-x-on-micrometer" class="heading-element">Use Prometheus client v1.x on Micrometer</h4><a id="user-content-use-prometheus-client-v1x-on-micrometer" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Use Prometheus client v1.x on Micrometer" href="#use-prometheus-client-v1x-on-micrometer"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>We have stopped using the simple client. All changes are based on: <a href="https://github.com/micrometer-metrics/micrometer/wiki/1.13-Migration-Guide">https://github.com/micrometer-metrics/micrometer/wiki/1.13-Migration-Guide</a>. Namely:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Counters now use Longs instead of Doubles.</p>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Before: `"registry="prometheus",status="200",uri="/example/prime/{number}"} 2.0 `</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Now: `"registry="prometheus",status="200",uri="/example/prime/{number}"} 2 `</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>io.micrometer.prometheusmetrics.PrometheusMeterRegistry</code> must be used instead of the old <code>io.micrometer.prometheus.PrometheusMeterRegistry</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>io.prometheus.metrics.tracer.common.SpanContext</code> must be used instead of the old <code>io.prometheus.client.exemplars.ExemplarSampler</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>The new Prometheus client uses some reserved words that must not be used to name metrics, therefore some metrics were renamed. Some examples of reserved words and renamed
metrics:</p>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><code>info</code>. Before: <code>jvm_info_total</code>. Now: <code>jvm_total</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>duration</code>. Before: <code>http_server_requests_duration_seconds</code>. Now: <code>http_server_requests_seconds</code></p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<p>Some metrics would display Tags ending in a comma (,). This trailing comma has now been removed. Example:
<code>"hibernate_flushes_total{entityManagerFactory=\"&lt;default&gt;\",env=\"test\",env2=\"test\",registry=\"prometheus\",} 1.0"</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p>It is no longer possible to create a metric generating Service Level Objectives and Percentiles at the same time.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-micrometer-registries-and-opentelemetry-metrics" class="heading-element">Micrometer registries and OpenTelemetry metrics</h4><a id="user-content-micrometer-registries-and-opentelemetry-metrics" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Micrometer registries and OpenTelemetry metrics" href="#micrometer-registries-and-opentelemetry-metrics"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>OpenTelemetry metrics is now enabled by default when the <code>quarkus-opentelemetry</code> or <code>quarkus-micrometer-opentelemetry</code> are set as dependencies of the project.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If any other Micrometer registry is present, like the popular <code>quarkus-micrometer-registry-prometheus</code>, a warning will be thrown at build time stating:</p>
</div>
<div>

<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[WARNING] [io.quarkus.micrometer.deployment.MicrometerProcessor] Redundant metrics collection detected: both Micrometer (registries: PrometheusMeterRegistry) and OpenTelemetry are active. Consider disabling one system (e.g., setting quarkus.otel.metrics.enabled=false)."><pre lang="log" class="notranslate"><code>[WARNING] [io.quarkus.micrometer.deployment.MicrometerProcessor] Redundant metrics collection detected: both Micrometer (registries: PrometheusMeterRegistry) and OpenTelemetry are active. Consider disabling one system (e.g., setting quarkus.otel.metrics.enabled=false).</code></pre></div>

The user might want metrics produced in both systems but usually this is not the case and we added this reminder.
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-opentelemetry" class="heading-element">OpenTelemetry</h3><a id="user-content-opentelemetry" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenTelemetry" href="#opentelemetry"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-opentelemetry-metrics-and-logs-are-now-stable-and-enabled-by-default" class="heading-element">OpenTelemetry Metrics and Logs are now stable and enabled by default</h4><a id="user-content-opentelemetry-metrics-and-logs-are-now-stable-and-enabled-by-default" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenTelemetry Metrics and Logs are now stable and enabled by default" href="#opentelemetry-metrics-and-logs-are-now-stable-and-enabled-by-default"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>This also makes <code>quarkus-micrometer-opentelemetry</code> stable. This includes the Micrometer to OpenTelemetry metrics bridge.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Please mind that using the <code>quarkus-micrometer-registry-prometheus</code> along with <code>quarkus-opentelemetry</code> will now produce independent Micrometer metrics and OpenTelemetry metrics. If you don’t need the later, you can disable it explicitly at build time with:</p>
</div>
<div>

<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="quarkus.otel.metrics.enabled=false"><pre lang="" class="notranslate"><code>quarkus.otel.metrics.enabled=false</code></pre></div>

</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-the-default-opentelemetry-otlp-protocol-changed-to-httpprotobuf" class="heading-element">The default OpenTelemetry OTLP protocol changed to <code>http/protobuf</code>
</h4><a id="user-content-the-default-opentelemetry-otlp-protocol-changed-to-httpprotobuf" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The default OpenTelemetry OTLP protocol changed to http/protobuf" href="#the-default-opentelemetry-otlp-protocol-changed-to-httpprotobuf"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-what-changed" class="heading-element">What changed</h5><a id="user-content-what-changed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What changed" href="#what-changed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Starting with Quarkus 4.x, the default OTLP exporter protocol has changed from <code>grpc</code> to <code>http/protobuf</code>, and the default endpoint port from <code>4317</code> to <code>4318</code>. This aligns Quarkus with the
<a href="https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-specification/blob/v1.28.0/specification/protocol/exporter.md#specify-protocol">OpenTelemetry specification</a> and its
<a href="https://github.com/open-telemetry/opentelemetry-java-instrumentation/pull/9993">Java implementation.</a></p>
</div>
<table role="table">
<colgroup>
<col>
<col>
<col>
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Before</th>
<th>After</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><p>Default protocol</p></td>
<td><p><code>grpc</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>http/protobuf</code></p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p>Default endpoint</p></td>
<td><p><code>http://localhost:4317</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>http://localhost:4318</code></p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<div>
<table role="table">
<tbody><tr>
<td>
<div>Warning</div>
</td>
<td>
<strong>Protocol and port must match.</strong>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-who-is-affected" class="heading-element">Who is affected</h5><a id="user-content-who-is-affected" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Who is affected" href="#who-is-affected"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Applications that rely on the <strong>default</strong> OTLP exporter configuration (i.e., no explicit <code>quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.protocol</code> or <code>quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.endpoint</code> set)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Applications sending telemetry to a collector that only listens on the gRPC port (4317)</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you already set <code>quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.protocol</code> and <code>quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.endpoint</code> explicitly, <strong>no action is required</strong>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-how-to-migrate" class="heading-element">How to migrate</h5><a id="user-content-how-to-migrate" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How to migrate" href="#how-to-migrate"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h6 id="user-content-option-1-accept-the-new-default-recommended" class="heading-element">Option 1: Accept the new default (recommended)</h6><a id="user-content-option-1-accept-the-new-default-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Option 1: Accept the new default (recommended)" href="#option-1-accept-the-new-default-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>If your OTLP collector supports <code>http/protobuf</code> on port 4318 (most modern collectors do), no configuration change is needed. Just verify your collector is listening on port 4318.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h6 id="user-content-option-2-keep-using-grpc" class="heading-element">Option 2: Keep using gRPC</h6><a id="user-content-option-2-keep-using-grpc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Option 2: Keep using gRPC" href="#option-2-keep-using-grpc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>If you need to continue using gRPC, explicitly set the protocol and endpoint:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# To all signals:

quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.protocol=grpc
quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.endpoint=http://localhost:4317

# Or per-signal:

quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.traces.protocol=grpc
quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.traces.endpoint=http://localhost:4317

quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.metrics.protocol=grpc
quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.metrics.endpoint=http://localhost:4317

quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.logs.protocol=grpc
quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.logs.endpoint=http://localhost:4317"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> To all signals:</span>

<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.protocol</span>=grpc
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.endpoint</span>=http://localhost:4317

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Or per-signal:</span>

<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.traces.protocol</span>=grpc
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.traces.endpoint</span>=http://localhost:4317

<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.metrics.protocol</span>=grpc
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.metrics.endpoint</span>=http://localhost:4317

<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.logs.protocol</span>=grpc
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.logs.endpoint</span>=http://localhost:4317</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-misconfiguration-example" class="heading-element">Misconfiguration example</h5><a id="user-content-misconfiguration-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Misconfiguration example" href="#misconfiguration-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>After upgrading, if you see warnings like this in your application logs, your exporter protocol and collector port might be mismatched:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="WARN  [io.qua.ope.run.exp.otl.sen.VertxHttpSender] Failed to export spans. The request could not be executed. Full error message: Connection refused: localhost/127.0.0.1:4318"><pre lang="log" class="notranslate"><code>WARN  [io.qua.ope.run.exp.otl.sen.VertxHttpSender] Failed to export spans. The request could not be executed. Full error message: Connection refused: localhost/127.0.0.1:4318</code></pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>This means your collector is likely still listening only on port <code>4317</code> (gRPC). Either:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Configure your collector to also accept <code>http/protobuf</code> on port <code>4318</code>, or</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Explicitly set the Quarkus exporter back to gRPC</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-sampling" class="heading-element">Sampling</h4><a id="user-content-sampling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Sampling" href="#sampling"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Traces sampling in production and test code has changes from 100% to just 10%, according to current best practices.</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>The <code>quarkus.otel.traces.sampler</code> default value was changed from <code>parentbased_always_on</code> to <code>parentbased_traceidratio</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>The <code>quarkus.otel.traces.sampler.arg</code> default value was changed from <code>1.0d</code> (100%) to <code>0.1d</code> (10%). Please mind that <code>quarkus.otel.traces.sampler.arg</code> works in tandem with <code>quarkus.otel.traces.sampler</code>, when set to <code>parentbased_traceidratio</code></p>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Do note that for dev mode, the default value remains at <code>1.0d</code> (100%)</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>To change back tracing sampling to 100%, this <strong>build time</strong> configuration can be added:
<code>quarkus.otel.traces.sampler=parentbased_always_on</code>
This will replace the sampler being used to set sampling allways on but conditioned by the parent. If the parent span was sampled out, the current will also not be sampled.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>When using the <code>parentbased_traceidratio</code> sampler, you can change the sampling ratio with the following <strong>runtime</strong> configuration, back to 100%:
<code>quarkus.otel.traces.sampler.arg=1.0d</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-opentelemetry-semantic-convention-updates" class="heading-element">OpenTelemetry Semantic Convention Updates</h4><a id="user-content-opentelemetry-semantic-convention-updates" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OpenTelemetry Semantic Convention Updates" href="#opentelemetry-semantic-convention-updates"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>This release updates the Quarkus OpenTelemetry extension to align with the latest OpenTelemetry semantic conventions. Some deprecated code has been removed and if you have dashboards, alerts, or queries that filter on the attributes listed below, you must update them.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-grpc-rpc-attribute-changes" class="heading-element">gRPC / RPC Attribute Changes</h5><a id="user-content-grpc--rpc-attribute-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: gRPC / RPC Attribute Changes" href="#grpc--rpc-attribute-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<table role="table">
<colgroup>
<col>
<col>
<col>
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Old Attribute</th>
<th>New Attribute</th>
<th>Notes</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><p><code>rpc.system</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>rpc.system.name</code></p></td>
<td><p>Renamed upstream</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>rpc.grpc.status_code</code> (long)</p></td>
<td><p><code>rpc.response.status_code</code> (string)</p></td>
<td><p>Renamed, type changed from integer to string</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>rpc.service</code></p></td>
<td><p><em>(removed)</em></p></td>
<td><p>Service name is now part of <code>rpc.method</code></p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>rpc.method</code> = <code>"SayHello"</code></p></td>
<td><p><code>rpc.method</code> = <code>"helloworld.Greeter/SayHello"</code></p></td>
<td><p>Now contains the full qualified method name (<code>service/method</code>) instead of the bare method name</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<div>
<p><strong>Example — before:</strong></p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<pre>rpc.system = "grpc"
rpc.service = "helloworld.Greeter"
rpc.method = "SayHello"
rpc.grpc.status_code = 0</pre>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p><strong>Example — after:</strong></p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<pre>rpc.system.name = "grpc"
rpc.method = "helloworld.Greeter/SayHello"
rpc.response.status_code = "0"</pre>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p><strong>Grafana / Tempo query migration:</strong></p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Before</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<pre>{ span.rpc.system = "grpc" &amp;&amp; span.rpc.grpc.status_code != 0 }</pre>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>After</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<pre>{ span.rpc.system.name = "grpc" &amp;&amp; span.rpc.response.status_code != "0" }</pre>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-database-attribute-removals" class="heading-element">Database Attribute Removals</h5><a id="user-content-database-attribute-removals" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Database Attribute Removals" href="#database-attribute-removals"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The following deprecated database attributes are no longer emitted by the Redis and SQL client instrumentations:</p>
</div>
<table role="table">
<colgroup>
<col>
<col>
</colgroup>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Removed Attribute</th>
<th>Replacement</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><p><code>db.connection_string</code></p></td>
<td><p>Use <code>network.peer.address</code> + <code>network.peer.port</code> instead</p></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><p><code>db.user</code></p></td>
<td><p>No direct replacement — attribute was deprecated by OTel semconv</p></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-end-user-attribute-changes" class="heading-element">End User Attribute Changes</h5><a id="user-content-end-user-attribute-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: End User Attribute Changes" href="#end-user-attribute-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-resource-attribute-changes" class="heading-element">Resource Attribute Changes</h5><a id="user-content-resource-attribute-changes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Resource Attribute Changes" href="#resource-attribute-changes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-code-attribute-stabilization" class="heading-element">Code Attribute Stabilization</h5><a id="user-content-code-attribute-stabilization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Code Attribute Stabilization" href="#code-attribute-stabilization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p><code>code.function.name</code> and <code>code.line.number</code> have graduated from incubating to stable in the OTel semantic conventions. The attribute keys are unchanged — no action needed on your side. This only affects internal imports
within the Quarkus source.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-behaviour-of-quarkus-otel-traces-enabledfalse" class="heading-element">Behaviour of <code>quarkus.otel.traces.enabled=false</code>
</h4><a id="user-content-behaviour-of-quarkusoteltracesenabledfalse" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Behaviour of quarkus.otel.traces.enabled=false" href="#behaviour-of-quarkusoteltracesenabledfalse"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Previously, when <code>quarkus.otel.traces.enabled=false</code>, work would still be done in the tracer, spans with valid ids would be created but later dropped and never exported. This would allow performing tracing propagation and showing MDC tracing data in the logs.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Not anymore. A disabled tracer shouldn’t do any work.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If the previous behavior is still required, it can be achieved by setting <code>quarkus.otel.traces.exporter=none</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>This way telemetry will still be generated and contexts will still be propagated but nothing will be exported.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-micrometer-opentelemetry-bridge" class="heading-element">Micrometer OpenTelemetry Bridge</h3><a id="user-content-micrometer-opentelemetry-bridge" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Micrometer OpenTelemetry Bridge" href="#micrometer-opentelemetry-bridge"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>The default time unit for metrics, like the <code>http.server.request.duration</code> and <code>http.client.request.duration</code>, has been changed from milliseconds to <strong>seconds</strong>. This was done per the <a href="https://opentelemetry.io/docs/specs/semconv/http/http-metrics/#metric-httpserverrequestduration" rel="nofollow">OpenTelemetry document</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-resilience" class="heading-element">Resilience</h2><a id="user-content-resilience" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Resilience" href="#resilience"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-smallrye-fault-tolerance" class="heading-element">SmallRye Fault Tolerance</h3><a id="user-content-smallrye-fault-tolerance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: SmallRye Fault Tolerance" href="#smallrye-fault-tolerance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>SmallRye Fault Tolerance was updated to version 7.0, which removes several deprecated features. See the <a href="https://smallrye.io/blog/fault-tolerance-7-0-0/" rel="nofollow">release notes</a> to learn more.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Notably, SmallRye Fault Tolerance no longer recognizes the <code>@Blocking</code> and <code>@NonBlocking</code> annotations on guarded methods. Consequently, all guarded methods with one of these annotations are marked as invalid during Quarkus build, because these annotations may only be put on "entrypoints".</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you’re using the SmallRye Fault Tolerance <strong>non-compatible</strong> mode, which is the Quarkus default, all asynchronous methods are automatically treated as non-blocking, so you can just remove <code>@NonBlocking</code>. Instead of <code>@Blocking</code>, use MicroProfile Fault Tolerance’s <code>@Asynchronous</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you’re using the SmallRye Fault Tolerance <strong>compatible</strong> mode, use SmallRye Fault Tolerance’s <code>@AsynchronousNonBlocking</code> instead of <code>@NonBlocking</code>, and MicroProfile Fault Tolerance’s <code>@Asynchronous</code> instead of <code>@Blocking</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-security" class="heading-element">Security</h2><a id="user-content-security" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security" href="#security"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-oidc" class="heading-element">OIDC</h3><a id="user-content-oidc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OIDC" href="#oidc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-io-quarkus-oidc-oidctenantconfig-can-no-longer-be-created-directly" class="heading-element">
<code>io.quarkus.oidc.OidcTenantConfig</code> can no longer be created directly</h4><a id="user-content-ioquarkusoidcoidctenantconfig-can-no-longer-be-created-directly" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: io.quarkus.oidc.OidcTenantConfig can no longer be created directly" href="#ioquarkusoidcoidctenantconfig-can-no-longer-be-created-directly"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The deprecated <code>io.quarkus.oidc.OidcTenantConfig</code> constructors, its getters and setters have been removed.
Use <code>OidcTenantConfig.builder()</code> to create a tenant configuration and <code>OidcTenantConfig.builder(config)</code> to create a modified copy of an existing one.
Use accessor methods such as <code>config.tenantId()</code> to read configuration values.</p>
</div>
</div>
