<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-oidc-client" class="heading-element">OIDC Client</h3><a id="user-content-oidc-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OIDC Client" href="#oidc-client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-deprecated-io-quarkus-oidc-client-oidcclientconfig-class-removed" class="heading-element">Deprecated <code>io.quarkus.oidc.client.OidcClientConfig</code> class removed</h4><a id="user-content-deprecated-ioquarkusoidcclientoidcclientconfig-class-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deprecated io.quarkus.oidc.client.OidcClientConfig class removed" href="#deprecated-ioquarkusoidcclientoidcclientconfig-class-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Deprecated legacy <code>io.quarkus.oidc.client.OidcClientConfig</code> configuration class has been removed from the <code>quarkus-oidc-client</code> extension.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>When creating OIDC clients programmatically, use the <code>io.quarkus.oidc.client.runtime.OidcClientConfig</code> builder, as described in the <a href="https://quarkus.io/guides/security-openid-connect-client-reference#use-oidc-clients" rel="nofollow">Use OidcClients</a> section of the OpenID Connect Client Reference guide.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p><strong>Before:</strong>

</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import io.quarkus.oidc.client.OidcClientConfig;

OidcClientConfig oidcClientConfig = new OidcClientConfig();
oidcClientConfig.setClientId(&quot;client&quot;);
oidcClientConfig.setId(&quot;clientId&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">io</span>.<span class="pl-s1">quarkus</span>.<span class="pl-s1">oidc</span>.<span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-s1">OidcClientConfig</span>;

<span class="pl-smi">OidcClientConfig</span> <span class="pl-s1">oidcClientConfig</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">OidcClientConfig</span>();
<span class="pl-s1">oidcClientConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">setClientId</span>(<span class="pl-s">"client"</span>);
<span class="pl-s1">oidcClientConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">setId</span>(<span class="pl-s">"clientId"</span>);</pre></div>

</div>
<div>
<p><strong>After:</strong>

</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="import io.quarkus.oidc.client.runtime.OidcClientConfig;

OidcClientConfig oidcClientConfig = OidcClientConfig.builder()
        .clientId(&quot;client&quot;)
        .id(&quot;clientId&quot;)
        .build();"><pre><span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">io</span>.<span class="pl-s1">quarkus</span>.<span class="pl-s1">oidc</span>.<span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-s1">runtime</span>.<span class="pl-s1">OidcClientConfig</span>;

<span class="pl-smi">OidcClientConfig</span> <span class="pl-s1">oidcClientConfig</span> = <span class="pl-smi">OidcClientConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">builder</span>()
        .<span class="pl-en">clientId</span>(<span class="pl-s">"client"</span>)
        .<span class="pl-en">id</span>(<span class="pl-s">"clientId"</span>)
        .<span class="pl-en">build</span>();</pre></div>

</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-deprecated-resteasy-io-quarkus-oidc-client-filter-oidcclientrequestfilter-class-removed" class="heading-element">Deprecated RESTEasy <code>io.quarkus.oidc.client.filter.OidcClientRequestFilter</code> class removed</h4><a id="user-content-deprecated-resteasy-ioquarkusoidcclientfilteroidcclientrequestfilter-class-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deprecated RESTEasy io.quarkus.oidc.client.filter.OidcClientRequestFilter class removed" href="#deprecated-resteasy-ioquarkusoidcclientfilteroidcclientrequestfilter-class-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Deprecated RESTEasy <code>io.quarkus.oidc.client.filter.OidcClientRequestFilter</code> class was removed from the <code>quarkus-resteasy-client-oidc-filter</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you already use an <code>io.quarkus.oidc.client.filter.OidcClientFilter</code> annotation, your application is not impacted.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you explicitly register an OIDC client request filter by listing this deprecated class with <code>@RegisterProvider(io.quarkus.oidc.client.filter.OidcClientRequestFilter)</code> then either switch to <code>@OidcClientFilter</code> or <code>@RegisterProvider(io.quarkus.oidc.client.resteasy.filter.OidcClientRequestFilter)</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-elytron-security" class="heading-element">Elytron Security</h3><a id="user-content-elytron-security" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Elytron Security" href="#elytron-security"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-default-realm-when-multiple-realms-are-enabled" class="heading-element">Default realm when multiple realms are enabled</h4><a id="user-content-default-realm-when-multiple-realms-are-enabled" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Default realm when multiple realms are enabled" href="#default-realm-when-multiple-realms-are-enabled"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>When more than one Elytron security realm is enabled, for example, the embedded realm together with the JDBC realm, the realm named <code>Quarkus</code> is now the default realm. To select another default realm, set the <code>quarkus.security.elytron.default-realm-name</code> configuration property. If no enabled realm has the configured default realm name, the application fails to start.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Additionally, each enabled realm must have a unique name, otherwise, the build fails.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-keycloak-admin-client" class="heading-element">Keycloak Admin Client</h3><a id="user-content-keycloak-admin-client" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Keycloak Admin Client" href="#keycloak-admin-client"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The Keycloak Admin Client extensions and <code>quarkus-test-keycloak-server</code> now use <code>keycloak-admin-client-jackson3</code>, the Jackson 3 variant of the Keycloak Admin Client. The Quarkus BOM manages this artifact instead of <code>keycloak-admin-client</code>. The Jackson 3 variant does not contain the <code>org.keycloak.util.JsonSerialization</code> class. Use <code>org.keycloak.json.KeycloakJsonMapperFactory.mapper()</code> instead.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-spring-boot-properties" class="heading-element">Spring Boot Properties</h2><a id="user-content-spring-boot-properties" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Spring Boot Properties" href="#spring-boot-properties"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<p>The <code>quarkus-spring-boot-properties</code> extension now binds <code>@ConfigurationProperties</code> classes with SmallRye Config mappings, the same mechanism used by <code>@ConfigMapping</code>. Everything supported by <code>@ConfigMapping</code> is now supported by <code>@ConfigurationProperties</code>, including nested classes, <code>Optional</code>, <code>List</code>, and <code>Map</code> fields with the regular configuration syntax, with some breaking changes to align Quarkus and Spring behaviors.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-interfaces-are-no-longer-supported" class="heading-element">Interfaces are no longer supported</h3><a id="user-content-interfaces-are-no-longer-supported" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Interfaces are no longer supported" href="#interfaces-are-no-longer-supported"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Spring Boot does not bind <code>@ConfigurationProperties</code> to interfaces, and Quarkus no longer does either. The build fails if you annotate an interface with <code>@ConfigurationProperties</code>. Convert the interface to a class, or annotate it with <code>@io.smallrye.config.ConfigMapping</code> instead.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-naming-strategy" class="heading-element">Naming strategy</h3><a id="user-content-naming-strategy" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Naming strategy" href="#naming-strategy"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>quarkus.spring-boot-properties.configuration-properties-naming-strategy</code> configuration property has been removed. Property names are kebab-case by default, so a <code>maxAttempts</code> field is read from <code>greeting.max-attempts</code>. To use another strategy, annotate the class with <code>@io.smallrye.config.ConfigMapping</code>:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Before, with quarkus.spring-boot-properties.configuration-properties-naming-strategy=verbatim
@ConfigurationProperties(&quot;greeting&quot;)
public class GreetingProperties {
    public int maxAttempts;
}

// After
@ConfigurationProperties(&quot;greeting&quot;)
@ConfigMapping(namingStrategy = NamingStrategy.VERBATIM)
public class GreetingProperties {
    public int maxAttempts;
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Before, with quarkus.spring-boot-properties.configuration-properties-naming-strategy=verbatim</span>
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">ConfigurationProperties</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting"</span>)
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">GreetingProperties</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">maxAttempts</span>;
}

<span class="pl-c">// After</span>
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">ConfigurationProperties</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting"</span>)
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">ConfigMapping</span>(<span class="pl-s1">namingStrategy</span> = <span class="pl-smi">NamingStrategy</span>.<span class="pl-c1">VERBATIM</span>)
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-smi">GreetingProperties</span> {
    <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">maxAttempts</span>;
}</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>A nested class declared inside the annotated class inherits its naming strategy and may declare its own <code>@ConfigMapping(namingStrategy = …​)</code>. A property class that is not enclosed by the annotated class is mapped in kebab-case, unless it declares a naming strategy itself.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Field names can be overridden with the Spring Boot <code>@org.springframework.boot.context.properties.bind.Name</code> annotation, or with the SmallRye Config <code>@WithName</code> and <code>@WithDefault</code> annotations. The MicroProfile <code>@ConfigProperty</code> annotation is ignored (it was used previously to set the name and default).</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-unknown-properties" class="heading-element">Unknown properties</h3><a id="user-content-unknown-properties" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Unknown properties" href="#unknown-properties"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p><code>ignoreUnknownFields</code> changes meaning. Before, <code>ignoreUnknownFields = false</code> failed the build for a field that had no public setter and was not a public non-final field. It now has its Spring Boot meaning: a configuration property under the class prefix that does not map to any field fails the application startup.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-fields-and-values" class="heading-element">Fields and values</h3><a id="user-content-fields-and-values" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Fields and values" href="#fields-and-values"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Fields are written directly and setters are no longer called. Logic in a setter does not run, and private fields without a setter, which were previously skipped with a warning, are now populated. <code>final</code> fields are still skipped, now without a warning.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Primitive fields without an initializer are now required. Previously, a missing property left an <code>int</code> or <code>boolean</code> field at its JVM default. Add an initializer to keep a default, or use a wrapper or <code>Optional</code> type.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>A class bound from configuration needs a no-arg constructor. A class returned from a <code>@Bean</code> method is used as the template of the mapping, so its constructor may take arguments, and its field values become the defaults.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>A nested class type is bound as a group of properties. A registered MicroProfile <code>Converter</code> for that type is no longer used, and inline values such as <code>servers.one=host: localhost, port: 8080</code> are no longer parsed. Use nested property names instead, <code>servers.one.host=localhost</code> and <code>servers.one.port=8080</code>. A nested type without a no-arg constructor is treated as a single value and requires a <code>Converter</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Lists of objects, previously only configurable in YAML, are now also supported with indexed properties, such as <code>nodes[0].name=main</code>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-instances" class="heading-element">Instances</h3><a id="user-content-instances" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Instances" href="#instances"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>One instance is created per class and prefix and shared between injection points, instead of a new instance per injection point. Bean Validation constraints on the class are still validated at startup.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-spring-test" class="heading-element">Spring Test</h2><a id="user-content-spring-test" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Spring Test" href="#spring-test"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<p>The <code>quarkus-test-spring</code> artifact, providing <code>@SpringBootTest</code> support, has been renamed to <code>quarkus-spring-test</code> to align with the naming of the other Spring compatibility artifacts.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>A relocation is in place so builds using <code>quarkus-test-spring</code> keep working for now, but you should update the artifactId in your build file:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;dependency&gt;
    &lt;groupId&gt;io.quarkus&lt;/groupId&gt;
    &lt;artifactId&gt;quarkus-spring-test&lt;/artifactId&gt;
    &lt;scope&gt;test&lt;/scope&gt;
&lt;/dependency&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">groupId</span>&gt;io.quarkus&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">groupId</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;quarkus-spring-test&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;test&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>or, with Gradle:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-groovy-gradle notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="testImplementation(&quot;io.quarkus:quarkus-spring-test&quot;)"><pre>testImplementation(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>io.quarkus:quarkus-spring-test<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>)</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-misc" class="heading-element">Misc</h2><a id="user-content-misc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Misc" href="#misc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-scala" class="heading-element">Scala</h3><a id="user-content-scala" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scala" href="#scala"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The long deprecated Scala 2 extension present in the Core repository has been dropped.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If you’re interested in using Quarkus with Scala, there is a <a href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-scala3">Scala 3 extension in the Quarkiverse</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-websockets-next" class="heading-element">WebSockets Next</h2><a id="user-content-websockets-next" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: WebSockets Next" href="#websockets-next"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<p><code>BasicWebSocketConnector</code> no longer appends a trailing slash to the base URI path and now sends the base URI exactly as you provide it. A base URI whose path is <code>/endpoint</code> was previously sent as <code>/endpoint/</code> but is now sent as <code>/endpoint</code>, while a base URI without any path is still sent as <code>/</code>. If your server only matches paths that end with a slash, append the trailing slash to the base URI that you pass to the connector.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 id="user-content-avro" class="heading-element">Avro</h2><a id="user-content-avro" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Avro" href="#avro"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-avro-deserialization-now-validates-classes" class="heading-element">Avro deserialization now validates classes</h3><a id="user-content-avro-deserialization-now-validates-classes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Avro deserialization now validates classes" href="#avro-deserialization-now-validates-classes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Quarkus now uses Apache Avro 1.12.2.
Starting with this version, Avro checks every class against a global security validator before deserializing it.
If the class is not trusted, deserialization fails with a <code>java.lang.SecurityException</code>.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>The <code>quarkus-avro</code> extension comes in transitively with <code>quarkus-apicurio-registry-avro</code>, <code>quarkus-confluent-registry-avro</code> and <code>quarkus-messaging-pulsar</code>.
It installs a validator that automatically trusts the following classes:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Avro-generated classes, meaning those annotated with <code>@org.apache.avro.specific.AvroGenerated</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Protobuf-generated messages in applications that use <code>quarkus-grpc</code> or <code>quarkus-messaging-pulsar</code>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Avro’s built-in trusted types, such as boxed primitives, <code>String</code>, <code>BigDecimal</code> and <code>BigInteger</code>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>Applications that only deserialize Avro-generated classes do not need to change anything.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>If your application deserializes other classes through Avro, for example reflect-based POJOs, trust them with the new build-time properties:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ini notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Trust individual classes by fully qualified name (use `$` for nested classes, e.g. org.acme.Outer$Inner)
quarkus.avro.trusted-classes=org.acme.MyClass,org.acme.OtherClass

# Trust whole packages, including their sub-packages
quarkus.avro.trusted-packages=org.acme.model"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Trust individual classes by fully qualified name (use `$` for nested classes, e.g. org.acme.Outer$Inner)</span>
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.avro.trusted-classes</span>=org.acme.MyClass,org.acme.OtherClass

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Trust whole packages, including their sub-packages</span>
<span class="pl-k">quarkus.avro.trusted-packages</span>=org.acme.model</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>Because these are build-time properties, you need to rebuild the application after changing them.
At runtime, you can still use Avro’s own <code>org.apache.avro.SERIALIZABLE_CLASSES</code> and <code>org.apache.avro.SERIALIZABLE_PACKAGES</code> system properties to trust more classes.
If you use the Avro library without the <code>quarkus-avro</code> extension, those system properties are the only option.</p>
</div>
<div>
<table role="table">
<tbody><tr>
<td>
<div>Important</div>
</td>
<td>
<div>
<p>In <code>@QuarkusIntegrationTest</code> tests, the test code runs in a separate JVM that is not a Quarkus application.
The <code>quarkus.avro.trusted-*</code> properties and the automatic trust of Avro-generated classes only apply to the application under test.
They do not apply to the test JVM.
If your test code deserializes Avro records itself, for example when consuming messages with <code>KafkaCompanion</code>, you must trust those classes in the test JVM yourself.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>One way to do this is to set the system property in the Maven Failsafe configuration:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;plugin&gt;
    &lt;artifactId&gt;maven-failsafe-plugin&lt;/artifactId&gt;
    &lt;configuration&gt;
        &lt;systemPropertyVariables&gt;
            &lt;org.apache.avro.SERIALIZABLE_PACKAGES&gt;org.acme.schema&lt;/org.apache.avro.SERIALIZABLE_PACKAGES&gt;
        &lt;/systemPropertyVariables&gt;
    &lt;/configuration&gt;
&lt;/plugin&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">plugin</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;maven-failsafe-plugin&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;
    &lt;<span class="pl-ent">configuration</span>&gt;
        &lt;<span class="pl-ent">systemPropertyVariables</span>&gt;
            &lt;<span class="pl-ent">org</span>.apache.avro.SERIALIZABLE_PACKAGES&gt;org.acme.schema&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">org</span>.apache.avro.SERIALIZABLE_PACKAGES&gt;
        &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">systemPropertyVariables</span>&gt;
    &lt;/<span class="pl-ent">configuration</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">plugin</span>&gt;</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>The other way is to install a validator programmatically before the test consumes any messages, for example in a <code>@BeforeAll</code> method:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ClassSecurityValidator.setGlobal(ClassSecurityValidator.composite(
        ClassSecurityValidator.builder().add(MyRecord.class).build(),
        ClassSecurityValidator.getGlobal()));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">ClassSecurityValidator</span>.<span class="pl-en">setGlobal</span>(<span class="pl-smi">ClassSecurityValidator</span>.<span class="pl-en">composite</span>(
        <span class="pl-smi">ClassSecurityValidator</span>.<span class="pl-en">builder</span>().<span class="pl-en">add</span>(<span class="pl-smi">MyRecord</span>.<span class="pl-k">class</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>(),
        <span class="pl-smi">ClassSecurityValidator</span>.<span class="pl-en">getGlobal</span>()));</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p><code>@QuarkusTest</code> tests are not affected because they run inside the Quarkus application.</p>
</div>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody></table>
</div>
<div>
<p>See the <a href="https://quarkus.io/version/main/guides/kafka-schema-registry-avro#avro-deserialization-and-class-security" rel="nofollow">Avro deserialization and class security</a> section of the Kafka Schema Registry and Avro guide for details.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-quarkus-messaging-pulsar-now-depends-on-quarkus-avro" class="heading-element">
<code>quarkus-messaging-pulsar</code> now depends on <code>quarkus-avro</code>
</h3><a id="user-content-quarkus-messaging-pulsar-now-depends-on-quarkus-avro" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: quarkus-messaging-pulsar now depends on quarkus-avro" href="#quarkus-messaging-pulsar-now-depends-on-quarkus-avro"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>quarkus-messaging-pulsar</code> extension now pulls in <code>quarkus-avro</code> transitively.
This way, the Avro class security validator is configured for Pulsar applications.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-deprecated-code-removed-from-core" class="heading-element">Deprecated code removed from <code>core</code>
</h4><a id="user-content-deprecated-code-removed-from-core" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deprecated code removed from core" href="#deprecated-code-removed-from-core"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Several APIs that had been deprecated for over 12 months were removed.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-codegenprovider-inputextension-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>CodeGenProvider</code> - <code>inputExtension()</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-codegenprovider---inputextension-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CodeGenProvider - inputExtension() removed" href="#codegenprovider---inputextension-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>inputExtension()</code> method (single extension) has been removed from <code>io.quarkus.deployment.CodeGenProvider</code>.
The <code>inputExtensions()</code> method (array) was previously delegating to it; it now returns an empty array by default.</p>
</div>
<div>
<p>Override <code>inputExtensions()</code> instead of <code>inputExtension()</code>:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Before
@Override
public String inputExtension() {
    return &quot;acme&quot;;
}

// After
@Override
public String[] inputExtensions() {
    return new String[] { &quot;acme&quot; };
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Before</span>
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Override</span>
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-en">inputExtension</span>() {
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s">"acme"</span>;
}

<span class="pl-c">// After</span>
<span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">Override</span>
<span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">String</span>[] <span class="pl-en">inputExtensions</span>() {
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">String</span>[] { <span class="pl-s">"acme"</span> };
}</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-capability-smallrye_reactive_messaging-constant-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>Capability.SMALLRYE_REACTIVE_MESSAGING</code> constant removed</h5><a id="user-content-capabilitysmallrye_reactive_messaging-constant-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Capability.SMALLRYE_REACTIVE_MESSAGING constant removed" href="#capabilitysmallrye_reactive_messaging-constant-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-packagetypebuilditem-legacyjarrequiredbuilditem-and-uberjarrequiredbuilditem-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>PackageTypeBuildItem</code>, <code>LegacyJarRequiredBuildItem</code>, and <code>UberJarRequiredBuildItem</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-packagetypebuilditem-legacyjarrequiredbuilditem-and-uberjarrequiredbuilditem-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: PackageTypeBuildItem, LegacyJarRequiredBuildItem, and UberJarRequiredBuildItem removed" href="#packagetypebuilditem-legacyjarrequiredbuilditem-and-uberjarrequiredbuilditem-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The following build items in <code>io.quarkus.deployment.pkg.builditem</code> have been removed:</p>
</div>
<div>
<ul>
<li>
<p><code>PackageTypeBuildItem</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>LegacyJarRequiredBuildItem</code></p>
</li>
<li>
<p><code>UberJarRequiredBuildItem</code></p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
<div>
<p>These items were already ignored at runtime.
To force a specific JAR type, implement a configuration customizer that sets <code>quarkus.package.jar.type</code> to <code>legacy-jar</code> or <code>uber-jar</code>, and add a build-step validation that asserts the expected type is configured.</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-memorysizeconverter-removed" class="heading-element">

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-testwatchedfiles-method-signatures-changed" class="heading-element">
<code>TestWatchedFiles</code> method signatures changed</h5><a id="user-content-testwatchedfiles-method-signatures-changed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: TestWatchedFiles method signatures changed" href="#testwatchedfiles-method-signatures-changed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p><code>TestWatchedFiles.setWatchedFilePaths(Map&lt;String, Boolean&gt;)</code> and <code>TestWatchedFiles.setWatchedFilesListener(Consumer&lt;Map&lt;String, Boolean&gt;&gt;)</code> have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.dev.testing.TestWatchedFiles</code>.
Use <code>setWatchedFilePaths(Map, List)</code> and <code>setWatchedFilesListener(BiConsumer)</code> instead.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-new-avrotrustedclassbuilditem-for-classes-deserialized-through-avro" class="heading-element">New <code>AvroTrustedClassBuildItem</code> for classes deserialized through Avro</h4><a id="user-content-new-avrotrustedclassbuilditem-for-classes-deserialized-through-avro" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: New AvroTrustedClassBuildItem for classes deserialized through Avro" href="#new-avrotrustedclassbuilditem-for-classes-deserialized-through-avro"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Since Avro 1.12.2, Avro rejects untrusted classes during deserialization.
If your extension generates or handles classes that are deserialized through Avro, mark them as trusted.
Add a dependency on <code>io.quarkus:quarkus-avro-spi</code> to your deployment module and produce an <code>io.quarkus.avro.spi.AvroTrustedClassBuildItem</code> for those classes.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@BuildStep
AvroTrustedClassBuildItem trustMyClasses() {
    return new AvroTrustedClassBuildItem(List.of(&quot;org.acme.MyGeneratedClass&quot;));
}"><pre><span class="pl-c1">@</span><span class="pl-c1">BuildStep</span>
<span class="pl-smi">AvroTrustedClassBuildItem</span> <span class="pl-en">trustMyClasses</span>() {
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">AvroTrustedClassBuildItem</span>(<span class="pl-smi">List</span>.<span class="pl-en">of</span>(<span class="pl-s">"org.acme.MyGeneratedClass"</span>));
}</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<p>The build item has no effect if the <code>quarkus-avro</code> extension is not present, so your extension does not need to depend on <code>quarkus-avro</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-independent-projects" class="heading-element">Independent Projects</h3><a id="user-content-independent-projects" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Independent Projects" href="#independent-projects"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-deprecated-code-removed-from-independent-projects" class="heading-element">Deprecated code removed from <code>independent-projects</code>
</h4><a id="user-content-deprecated-code-removed-from-independent-projects" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deprecated code removed from independent-projects" href="#deprecated-code-removed-from-independent-projects"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Several APIs in the bootstrap, devtools, and ArC modules that had been deprecated for over 12 months were removed.
These are extension-author and tooling-author level APIs; typical application developers are not affected.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-quarkusbootstrap-builderpath-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>QuarkusBootstrap.builder(Path)</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-quarkusbootstrapbuilderpath-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: QuarkusBootstrap.builder(Path) removed" href="#quarkusbootstrapbuilderpath-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>builder(Path applicationRoot)</code> factory method has been removed from <code>io.quarkus.bootstrap.app.QuarkusBootstrap</code>.
Use <code>builder()</code> and set the application root explicitly:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Before
QuarkusBootstrap.builder(applicationRoot);

// After
QuarkusBootstrap.builder().setApplicationRoot(PathList.of(applicationRoot));"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Before</span>
<span class="pl-smi">QuarkusBootstrap</span>.<span class="pl-en">builder</span>(<span class="pl-s1">applicationRoot</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// After</span>
<span class="pl-smi">QuarkusBootstrap</span>.<span class="pl-en">builder</span>().<span class="pl-en">setApplicationRoot</span>(<span class="pl-smi">PathList</span>.<span class="pl-en">of</span>(<span class="pl-s1">applicationRoot</span>));</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-additionaldependency-getarchivepath-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>AdditionalDependency.getArchivePath()</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-additionaldependencygetarchivepath-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: AdditionalDependency.getArchivePath() removed" href="#additionaldependencygetarchivepath-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>getArchivePath()</code> method has been removed from <code>io.quarkus.bootstrap.app.AdditionalDependency</code>.
Use <code>getResolvedPaths()</code>, which returns an <code>io.quarkus.paths.PathCollection</code>, instead of the previously returned <code>PathsCollection</code>.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-capabilitycontract-deprecated-factory-and-constructor-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>CapabilityContract</code> deprecated factory and constructor removed</h5><a id="user-content-capabilitycontract-deprecated-factory-and-constructor-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CapabilityContract deprecated factory and constructor removed" href="#capabilitycontract-deprecated-factory-and-constructor-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>providesCapabilities(String, String)</code> static factory and the <code>CapabilityContract(String, Collection)</code> constructor have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.bootstrap.model.CapabilityContract</code>.
Use <code>of(String extension, String providesStr, String requiresStr)</code> and the <code>CapabilityContract(String, Collection, Collection)</code> constructor instead, passing <code>null</code>/<code>List.of()</code> for the requires argument:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Before
CapabilityContract.providesCapabilities(extension, provides);

// After
CapabilityContract.of(extension, provides, null);"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Before</span>
<span class="pl-smi">CapabilityContract</span>.<span class="pl-en">providesCapabilities</span>(<span class="pl-s1">extension</span>, <span class="pl-s1">provides</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// After</span>
<span class="pl-smi">CapabilityContract</span>.<span class="pl-en">of</span>(<span class="pl-s1">extension</span>, <span class="pl-s1">provides</span>, <span class="pl-c1">null</span>);</pre></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-artifactkey-gac-and-gact-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>ArtifactKey.gac()</code> and <code>gact()</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-artifactkeygac-and-gact-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ArtifactKey.gac() and gact() removed" href="#artifactkeygac-and-gact-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>gac(String, String, String)</code> and <code>gact(String, String, String, String)</code> static factory methods have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.maven.dependency.ArtifactKey</code>.
Use <code>of(String groupId, String artifactId, String classifier, String type)</code> instead:</p>
</div>
<div>
<div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Before
ArtifactKey.gac(groupId, artifactId, classifier);
ArtifactKey.gact(groupId, artifactId, classifier, type);

// After
ArtifactKey.of(groupId, artifactId, classifier, null);
ArtifactKey.of(groupId, artifactId, classifier, type);"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Before</span>
<span class="pl-smi">ArtifactKey</span>.<span class="pl-en">gac</span>(<span class="pl-s1">groupId</span>, <span class="pl-s1">artifactId</span>, <span class="pl-s1">classifier</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// After</span>
<span class="pl-smi">ArtifactKey</span>.<span class="pl-en">of</span>(<span class="pl-s1">groupId</span>, <span class="pl-s1">artifactId</span>, <span class="pl-s1">classifier</span>, <span class="pl-c1">null</span>);

<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-bootstrapdebug-constants-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>BootstrapDebug</code> constants removed</h5><a id="user-content-bootstrapdebug-constants-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: BootstrapDebug constants removed" href="#bootstrapdebug-constants-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>DEBUG_CLASSES_DIR</code>, <code>DEBUG_TRANSFORMED_CLASSES_DIR</code>, and <code>DEBUG_SOURCES_DIR</code> constants have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.bootstrap.BootstrapDebug</code>.
Use the <code>debugClassesDir()</code>, <code>transformedClassesDir()</code>, and <code>debugSourcesDir()</code> methods instead.
They are methods rather than constants so that the underlying system properties are re-read over the life of the JVM, which matters for tests.</p>
</div>
</div>

<div>
<p>The <code>quarkusMavenPluginVersion(String)</code> and <code>quarkusGradlePluginVersion(String)</code> methods have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.devtools.commands.CreateProject</code>.

<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-observerinfo-getid-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>ObserverInfo.getId()</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-observerinfogetid-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ObserverInfo.getId() removed" href="#observerinfogetid-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>getId()</code> method has been removed from <code>io.quarkus.arc.processor.ObserverInfo</code>.
Use <code>getUserId()</code> instead.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 id="user-content-devtools" class="heading-element">Devtools</h3><a id="user-content-devtools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Devtools" href="#devtools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 id="user-content-deprecated-code-removed-from-devtools" class="heading-element">Deprecated code removed from <code>devtools</code>
</h4><a id="user-content-deprecated-code-removed-from-devtools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deprecated code removed from devtools" href="#deprecated-code-removed-from-devtools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>Two Maven plugin-tooling helper methods that had been deprecated for over 12 months were removed.
These are extension-author and tooling-author level APIs; typical application developers are not affected.</p>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-quarkusbootstrapmojo-repositorysystem-and-remoterepositorymanager-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>QuarkusBootstrapMojo.repositorySystem()</code> and <code>remoteRepositoryManager()</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-quarkusbootstrapmojorepositorysystem-and-remoterepositorymanager-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: QuarkusBootstrapMojo.repositorySystem() and remoteRepositoryManager() removed" href="#quarkusbootstrapmojorepositorysystem-and-remoterepositorymanager-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>repositorySystem()</code> and <code>remoteRepositoryManager()</code> methods have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.maven.QuarkusBootstrapMojo</code>.
Use <code>repositorySystemSession()</code>, which is still available, to access the underlying Aether repository system session instead.</p>
</div>
</div>
<div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h5 id="user-content-quarkusbootstrapprovider-repositorysystem-and-remoterepositorymanager-removed" class="heading-element">
<code>QuarkusBootstrapProvider.repositorySystem()</code> and <code>remoteRepositoryManager()</code> removed</h5><a id="user-content-quarkusbootstrapproviderrepositorysystem-and-remoterepositorymanager-removed" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: QuarkusBootstrapProvider.repositorySystem() and remoteRepositoryManager() removed" href="#quarkusbootstrapproviderrepositorysystem-and-remoterepositorymanager-removed"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div>
<p>The <code>repositorySystem()</code> and <code>remoteRepositoryManager()</code> methods have been removed from <code>io.quarkus.maven.QuarkusBootstrapProvider</code>.
There is no direct replacement; these were internal helpers exposing Aether internals that are no longer needed now that dependency resolution goes through the workspace-aware artifact resolver.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
              </div>
